i know that might be sound weird just to

02:59:54 --> 03:00:00
hear but it's it was a huge honor to

02:59:57 --> 03:00:02
talk to you

02:59:59 --> 03:00:05
i hope we talk again thank you so much

03:00:01 --> 03:00:06
for your time thank you so much

03:00:04 --> 03:00:08
thanks for listening to this

03:00:05 --> 03:00:11
conversation with eugenia cuida and

03:00:07 --> 03:00:14
thank you to our sponsors doordash

03:00:10 --> 03:00:16
dollar shave club and cash app click the

03:00:13 --> 03:00:18

03:00:15 --> 03:00:20

03:00:18 --> 03:00:23
if you enjoy this thing subscribe on

03:00:19 --> 03:00:26
youtube review 5 stars on apple podcast

03:00:22 --> 03:00:28
follow on spotify support on patreon or

03:00:25 --> 03:00:30
connect with me on twitter at lex

03:00:27 --> 03:00:33
friedman and now let me leave you with

03:00:29 --> 03:00:35
some words from carl sagan

03:00:32 --> 03:00:37
the world is so exquisite with so much

03:00:34 --> 03:00:39
love and moral depth that there's no

03:00:36 --> 03:00:40
reason to deceive ourselves with pretty

03:00:38 --> 03:00:42
stories of which there's little good

03:00:40 --> 03:00:44
evidence

03:00:41 --> 03:00:46
far better it seems to me and our

03:00:43 --> 03:00:48
vulnerability

03:00:45 --> 03:00:51
is to look death in the eye and to be

03:00:47 --> 03:00:53
grateful every day for the brief but

03:00:50 --> 03:00:55
magnificent opportunity that life

03:00:52 --> 03:00:57
provides

03:00:54 --> 03:01:01
thank you for listening and hope to see

03:00:56 --> 03:01:01
